- **hookfrisk**: Frisk your agent hooks for commands that auto-run on untrusted input before you ship them. Triggers automatically when you are about to report a coding task done and your diff added or changed a hook (or on /hookfrisk). A hook fires by itself, with no human in the loop — so a hook that pipes tool output or a file path into a shell, or runs curl | bash, is remote code execution on every trigger. hookfrisk reads only the changed hook definitions, flags the ones that run attacker-influenced input or unsandboxed commands, proposes the safe rewrite, and refuses to say "done" while an auto-firing h… ([ryanda9910/hookfrisk](https://github.com/ryanda9910/hookfrisk/tree/HEAD/skill))
